## Theorem: exact marginal for a future pencil
Let v_1,...,v_m in Q^2 be nonzero pairwise nonparallel coefficient directions, m>=2, and I_i nonempty real intervals with arbitrary open/closed/unbounded endpoints. Suppose P={y in R^2 : v_i dot y in I_i for all i} is nonempty. Let w be a nonzero rational direction nonparallel to each v_i. For i<j put alpha_ij=det(w,v_j)/det(v_i,v_j), beta_ij=det(v_i,w)/det(v_i,v_j). Then the exact range of w dot y over P is
J_w = intersection_{i<j} (alpha_ij I_i + beta_ij I_j).
Each summand is an interval, with negative scaling swapping endpoints and flags, and finite Minkowski endpoint strictness given by OR. The result is a possibly unbounded interval, possibly a closed singleton. This works for mixed strict/weak words.

Proof: for a fixed real t, let H_t={y:w dot y=t}. The map y->(v_i dot y,v_j dot y) is invertible. Hence H_t meets the two corresponding strips simultaneously iff t belongs to the displayed pair interval. Necessity follows for all pairs. For sufficiency apply finite Helly in the auxiliary two-dimensional coefficient space to the strips and H_t. Triples of strips meet by the hypothesis P nonempty; triples containing H_t meet by the pair tests. Finite Helly for convex sets includes open sets, lines and degenerate strips (no compactness premise for a finite family). The case m=2 is the invertible map itself. Thus every t in J_w has a common slope witness. Empty-base feasibility must be checked before using this identity. No new native spatial plane is asserted.

Consequently appending any local word with exact slope interval I_new is slope-compatible iff I_new intersects J_w. R288 establishes that these are necessary tests for full coefficient realization. For a fixed existing prefix, the ten triple comparisons per sixth word can be factored into ten pair-interval intersections once, followed by one overlap per sixth word. Each finite extreme of J_w is attained as a bound from one pair of old strips, hence its provenance contains at most four original endpoint rows by R288. When I_new is disjoint, the opposing new bound has at most two rows; the resulting six-row certificate is valid already in the original five coefficients (sum with appropriate positive weights; at a touching endpoint at least one side must be strict). A finite bound tied by several pairs keeps a strict witness if any tied bound is strict. The source endpoints and all original words remain retained. The marginal is exact only for the next direction's readout and overlap; it must not be used as the full state for other directions, joint intercept constraints, germs, or BRC suffix capacity. Recompute the marginal from all current strips or keep the full coefficient state. No loss of the original 98-bit branch information.

## Executed exact computation
Decode R276 first text bank in its original order, expanding ascending fifth set bits. Filter R288's dictionary to strict words; verify every local word's order against R275's all-positive then l-major/h-major negative block order and all counts. All 3103 prefixes [5350,8453) evaluated with all 79 sixth strict words (zero-based). Exact BigInt rational arithmetic in coefficient space. Precompute pair images, intersect to get J_w for each prefix, then independently compare its two bounds and strictness with direct Fourier–Motzkin elimination of f from the five original slope strips for w=(1,0). If a strip lower bound is l_i and upper bound h_j, all U_i>0 here and the reference row is (U_j T_i-U_i T_j)e + U_i h_j-U_j l_i >= 0, strict when either bound is strict. Constant rows checked. This is a separate exact reference to the slope projection, not independent human review or a revalidation of full native BRC. Both executions agreed on all 3103 projections. ## Exact reproducer
Inputs dict=JSON.parse of R288's Complete local interval cache JSON block; bank=R276 first text block trimmed. Call compute(dict,bank). All source provenance preserved. Mask bits set iff slope-compatible.
```javascript
function compute(dict,bank){
 const abs=a=>a<0n?-a:a,gcd=(a,b)=>b?gcd(b,a%b):abs(a);
 const R=(a,b=1n)=>{if(!b)throw Error("zero denominator");let d=gcd(a,b)*(b<0n?-1n:1n);return [a/d,b/d]},add=(a,b)=>R(a[0]*b[1]+b[0]*a[1],a[1]*b[1]),mul=(a,b)=>R(a[0]*b[0],a[1]*b[1]),neg=a=>[-a[0],a[1]],cmp=(a,b)=>{let d=a[0]*b[1]-b[0]*a[1];return d>0n?1:d<0n?-1:0},fmt=a=>a.map(String).join("/");
 const dirs=[[0n,1n],[1n,9n],[1n,4n],[2n,5n],[1n,17n],[1n,0n]];
 const B=x=>x?{v:R(...x.value.split("/").map(BigInt)),s:x.strict,src:x.endpoints}:null;
 const W=dict.slice(0,6).map(ws=>ws.filter(w=>!w.word.split(",").includes("0")).map(w=>({lo:B(w.lo),hi:B(w.hi),word:w.word})));
 if(W.map(w=>w.length).join()!=="11,7,16,22,46,79")throw Error("pattern counts");
 for(let k=0;k<6;k++){let n=W[k][0].word.split(",").length,words=[Array(n).fill(1).join()];for(let l=0;l<n;l++)for(let h=l;h<n;h++)words.push(Array.from({length:n},(_,i)=>l<=i&&i<=h?-1:1).join());if(words.some((w,i)=>w!==W[k][i].word))throw Error("pattern order")}
 const tight=(a,b,lower)=>{if(!a)return b;if(!b)return a;let c=cmp(a.v,b.v)*(lower?1:-1);return c>0?a:c<0?b:a.s?a:b};
 const ok=(lo,hi)=>!lo||!hi||cmp(lo.v,hi.v)<0||cmp(lo.v,hi.v)===0&&!lo.s&&!hi.s;
 const equal=(a,b)=>!a&&!b||!!a&&!!b&&cmp(a.v,b.v)===0&&a.s===b.s;
 const det=(a,b)=>a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0];
 const scale=(w,c)=>{let t=x=>x?{v:mul(x.v,c),s:x.s,src:x.src}:null;return c[0]>0n?{lo:t(w.lo),hi:t(w.hi)}:{lo:t(w.hi),hi:t(w.lo)}};
 const sum=(x,y,p)=>x&&y?{v:add(x.v,y.v),s:x.s||y.s,src:[...x.src,...y.src],pair:p}:null;
 const pairs=[];let cacheEntries=0;for(let i=0;i<5;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<5;j++){let d=det(dirs[i],dirs[j]),a=R(det(dirs[5],dirs[j]),d),b=R(det(dirs[i],dirs[5]),d);let cache=W[i].map(x=>W[j].map(y=>{let X=scale(x,a),Y=scale(y,b);return {lo:sum(X.lo,Y.lo,[i,j]),hi:sum(X.hi,Y.hi,[i,j])}}));cacheEntries+=W[i].length*W[j].length;pairs.push({i,j,cache})}
 let words=[];const alphabet="0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";for(let line of bank.split("\n")){let [k,v]=line.split(":");let a=[...k].map(c=>alphabet.indexOf(c)),m=BigInt("0x"+v);if(a.length!==4||a.some((p,i)=>p<0||p>=W[i].length)||m<=0n||m>>46n)throw Error("bank decode");for(let j=0;j<46;j++)if(m&(1n<<BigInt(j)))words.push([...a,j])}if(words.length!==8453)throw Error("bank size");
 let st={prefixes:0,visits:0,rejected:0,passed:0,pairChecks:0,referencePairs:0,exactProjectionMatches:0,allPass:0,allReject:0,unbounded:0,oneSided:0,twoSided:0,distinctIntervals:0,distinctMasks:0,cacheEntries},out=[],intervals=new Set(),masks=new Set();
 for(let k=5350;k<words.length;k++){let a=words[k],lo=null,hi=null;for(let {i,j,cache} of pairs){let z=cache[a[i]][a[j]];lo=tight(lo,z.lo,true);hi=tight(hi,z.hi,false);st.pairChecks++}
 let L=null,H=null;for(let i=0;i<5;i++)for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=W[i][a[i]].lo,y=W[j][a[j]].hi;if(!x||!y)continue;st.referencePairs++;let [T,U]=dirs[i],[V,Z]=dirs[j],A=Z*T-U*V,C=add(mul(y.v,R(U)),neg(mul(x.v,R(Z)))),s=x.s||y.s;if(!A){let d=cmp(C,R(0n));if(d<0||d===0&&s)throw Error("old prefix infeasible");continue}let b={v:mul(neg(C),R(1n,A)),s};if(A>0n)L=tight(L,b,true);else H=tight(H,b,false)}
 if(!ok(lo,hi)||!equal(lo,L)||!equal(hi,H))throw Error("projection mismatch at "+k);st.exactProjectionMatches++;st.prefixes++;if(lo&&hi)st.twoSided++;else if(lo||hi)st.oneSided++;else st.unbounded++;
 let mask=0n;for(let j=0;j<79;j++){let w=W[5][j],pass=ok(tight(lo,w.lo,true),tight(hi,w.hi,false));st.visits++;if(pass){mask|=1n<<BigInt(j);st.passed++}else st.rejected++}
 if(mask===0n)st.allReject++;if(mask===(1n<<79n)-1n)st.allPass++;
 const bound=x=>x?`${fmt(x.v)}${x.s?"o":"c"}@${x.pair.join(",")}:${x.src.join(",")}`:"*";const id=x=>x?`${fmt(x.v)}${x.s?"o":"c"}`:"*";intervals.add(id(lo)+"|"+id(hi));masks.add(mask.toString(16));out.push(`${k}:${mask.toString(16)}:${bound(lo)}:${bound(hi)}`)}
 st.distinctIntervals=intervals.size;st.distinctMasks=masks.size;return {st,out};
}
```
